충남 서천군 한솔제지 장항공장에서 30대 근로자가 대형 종이롤에 끼여 숨졌다.11일 경찰과 소방 당국 등에 따르면 전날 오후 7시 20분경 서천 장항읍 한솔제지 장항공장 감열동 슬리터 공정에서 협력업체 소속 근로자가 작업 중 종이롤에 끼였다.이 사고로 30대 근로자는 크게 다쳐 병원에 옮겨져 치료를 받던 중 결국 숨졌다.사고 당시 피해자는 지게차로 종이롤을 옮기던 중 롤이 떨어져 굴러가자 이를 막으려다 철문과 롤 사이에 끼인 것으로 파악됐다.경찰은 현재 공장 관계자 등을 상대로 안전수칙 준수 여부 등 정확한 사고 경위를 조사 중이다.

A worker in his 30s was caught in a large paper roll at Janghang Factory in Hansol, Seocheon-gun, Chungcheongnam-do.According to the police and fire authorities, a worker belonging to a contractor was stuck in a paper roll while working at the thermal field slitter process at Janghang Factory in Hansol, Janghang-eup, Seochon, Chungcheongnam-do around 7:20 p.m. on the previous day. In this accident, a worker in his 30s was seriously injured and was taken to a hospital for treatment, where he eventually died. At the time of the accident, the victim was found to have been stuck between the iron gate and the roll when the roll fell and rolled while moving the paper roll with the forklift. Police are currently investigating the exact accident, including compliance with safety rules, against factory officials.
